; Only really here for Death Machine. For a dissassembly, see USA version.
; Maybe check for Hexas, Aega, and Mech Rider AI flaws.
; Of note, the USA version has 38 bytes of localization garbage at the very end of the bank.
; A quick scan of the top half of this bank seems like everything is the same.
; So probably the only changes to this bank from JP > USA is changing Death Machine's AI to Kettle Kin.

; JP Version of the Setup Header Pointer Table
;[PointerTable_StructuredBossSetupHeaders]

;D0/BC38: 0AD2 [02: Kilroy]
;D0/BC3A: C2DF [03: Frost Gigas, Fire Gigas, Thunder Gigas]
;D0/BC3C: D1E5 [04: Vampire, Buffy]
;D0/BC3E: 59DD [05: Tonpole, Biting Lizard, Snap Dragon]
;D0/BC40: F9D3 [06: Death Machine]
;D0/BC42: 6DEB [07: Mech Rider I, Mech Rider II, Mech Rider III]
;D0/BC44: 52E8 [08: Dark Lich]
;D0/BC46: 44E1 [09: Hexas]
;D0/BC48: 21ED [0A: Great Viper, Dragon Worm]
;D0/BC4A: 30F0 [0B: Red Dragon, Blue Dragon, Snow Dragon]
;D0/BC4C: 0EDB [0C: Spikey Tiger, Blue Spike]
;D0/BC4E: 19E3 [0D: Spring Beak, Axe Beak]
;D0/BC50: FCD8 [0E: Aegagropilon]
